Provides strategy-based cleaning operations including deduplication, missing value imputation, text processing, and outlier handling through a configurable pipeline.\n\n## Core Components\n\n### CleaningStrategies\nCollection of static methods for specific cleaning operations:\n\n#### Deduplication\n- `remove_duplicates(df, subset=None)` - Remove duplicate rows given the context of the prompt or the context of the dataset, Carefully evaluate if it still makes sense to allow duplicates exist in the downstream analysis. You should always check the data and remove them based on the logic of meta data and downstream goal.\n\n#### Missing Value Handling\nLogic: If the column is not critical to affect the dowstream task and the cost of dropping the whole line is not neccessary, you should try to impute them, otherwise you can drop.\n- `drop_missing(df, columns)` - Drop rows with missing critical values (that will affect the downstream task's peformance) in specified columns.\n- `impute_median(df, columns)` - Impute with median for numerical columns if the column is numerical data\n- `impute_knn(df, target_features, n_neighbors=5)` - KNN-based imputation such that the target column value potentially can be inferred by other columns. I.e. you have to carefully see the semantic and logical relationship between columns in terms of real life cases and make decisions to use this method for better data imputation performance.\n- `impute_mode(df, columns)` - Impute with mode for categorical columns if the target column is relatively independent to other columns and should just be filled with an acceptable value.\n\n#### Text Processing\n- `process_text(df, columns, operation)` - Text operations: some columns might need conduct text related processing to get numbers or remove unnecessary content, you need to dertermine if this is needed based on preview the column values regarding the down stream goal. (extract_numbers, clean_whitespace, extract_email, lowercase, remove_special)\n\n#### Outlier Handling\nLogic: Again you need to make sure if removing outliers is costly or not in terms of the sample size or critical columns under your investigation. Keep the rows by replacing the outlier value with a more reasonable value, or simply remove the whole row in one of the two ways if it barely has any impact.\n- `cap_outliers_iqr(df, columns, multiplier=1.5)` - Winsorization using IQR to keep reasonable signals of the data;\n- `remove_outliers_iqr(df, columns, multiplier=1.5)` - Remove outliers using IQR\n- `remove_outliers_zscore(df, columns, threshold=3.0)` - Remove outliers using Z-score\n\n### DataCleaningPipeline\nOrchestrates multiple cleaning steps with logging and progress tracking.\n\n## Usage\n\n```python\nfrom data_cleaning import CleaningStrategies, DataCleaningPipeline\n\n# Create pipeline\npipeline = DataCleaningPipeline(name=\"Transaction Data\")\n\n# Add cleaning steps\npipeline.add_step(\n    CleaningStrategies.remove_duplicates,\n    subset=['customer_id', 'order_date'],\n    description=\"Remove duplicate orders\"\n).add_step(\n    CleaningStrategies.drop_missing,\n    columns=['customer_id', 'amount'],\n    description=\"Drop rows with missing critical fields\"\n).add_step(\n    CleaningStrategies.impute_knn,\n    target_features={\n        'income': {'features': ['age', 'education'], 'type': 'numeric'}\n    },\n    description=\"KNN imputation for income\"\n).add_step(\n    CleaningStrategies.cap_outliers_iqr,\n    columns=['amount'],\n    multiplier=1.5,\n    description=\"Cap price outliers\"\n)\n\n# Execute pipeline\ndf_clean = pipeline.execute(raw_df, verbose=True)\n\n# Get execution log\nlog_df = pipeline.get_log()\n```\n\n## Input\n- Any tabular DataFrame requiring cleaning\n\n## Output\n- Cleaned DataFrame with execution log available\n\n## Best Practices\n- Chain multiple steps using method chaining\n- Use verbose=True to monitor pipeline progress\n- Review execution log for quality assurance\n- Extend CleaningStrategies with domain-specific methods as needed\n\n\nARGUMENTS: --input /app/data/survey_dirty.csv --output /app/output/survey_cleaned.csv"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"29be4f7a-89f9-48ae-a013-68ce3a2abfd6","uuid":"1e03ed3f-ccd6-42cf-ab45-01895a5e5ca0","isSynthetic":true}

Step 4: Handle missing Quantity\n    # Quantity is critical, drop these rows\n    pipeline.add_step(\n        CleaningStrategies.drop_missing,\n        columns=['Quantity'],\n        description=\"Drop rows with missing quantity\"\n    )\n\n    # Step 5: Remove outliers in Purchase Price Per Unit using IQR\n    # Cap outliers instead of removing to preserve sample size\n    pipeline.add_step(\n        CleaningStrategies.cap_outliers_iqr,\n        columns=['Purchase Price Per Unit'],\n        multiplier=3.0,  # More lenient to preserve variation\n        description=\"Cap price outliers using IQR\"\n    )\n\n    # Step 6: Remove outliers in Quantity using IQR\n    # Cap outliers instead of removing\n    pipeline.add_step(\n        CleaningStrategies.cap_outliers_iqr,\n        columns=['Quantity'],\n        multiplier=3.0,  # More lenient\n        description=\"Cap quantity outliers using IQR\"\n    )\n\n    # Step 7: Remove exact duplicate rows (all columns)\n    # Only remove if ALL fields are identical\n    pipeline.add_step(\n        CleaningStrategies.remove_duplicates,\n        subset=None,  # Execute pipeline\n    df_clean = pipeline.execute(df, verbose=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\nCleaned shape: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {df.shape[0] - df_clean.shape[0]}\")\n\n    # Additional data validation and transformation\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"ADDITIONAL DATA TRANSFORMATIONS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Convert Order Date to datetime\n    df_clean['Order Date'] = pd.to_datetime(df_clean['Order Date'], errors='coerce')\n    print(f\"\\nConverted Order Date to datetime\")\n\n    # Drop rows with invalid dates\n    before_count = len(df_clean)\n    df_clean = df_clean.dropna(subset=['Order Date'])\n    after_count = len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"Dropped {before_count - after_count} rows with invalid dates\")\n\n    # Calculate Total Spend = Purchase Price Per Unit * Quantity\n    df_clean['Total_Spend'] = df_clean['Purchase Price Per Unit'] * df_clean['Quantity']\n    print(f\"\\nCreated Total_Spend column\")\n\n    # Filter to 2019-2020 data only\n    df_clean = df_clean[\n        (df_clean['Order Date'] >= '2019-01-01') &\n        (df_clean['Order Date'] <= '2020-12-31')\n    ]\n    print(f\"Filtered to 2019-2020: {len(df_clean)} rows\")\n\n    # Show date range\n    print(f\"\\nDate range: {df_clean['Order Date'].min()} to {df_clean['Order Date'].max()}\")\n\n    # Save cleaned data\n    df_clean.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"\\nCleaned purchase data saved to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Show summary\n    print(\"\\nCleaned data summary:\")\n    print(df_clean.info())\n    print(\"\\nCategory distribution:\")\n    print(df_clean['Category'].value_counts().head(20))\n\n    return df_clean\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Prepare data for Prophet (needs 'ds' and 'y' columns)\n    df_prophet = category_data[['ds', 'y']].copy()\n\n    # Filter to data BEFORE treatment period for training\n    train_data = df_prophet[df_prophet['ds'] < treatment_start].copy()\n\n    # Need sufficient historical data\n    if len(train_data) < 30:\n        return {\n            'category': category_name,\n            'anomaly_index': 0,\n            'baseline_avg': 0,\n            'treatment_avg': 0,\n            'forecast_avg': 0,\n            'error': 'Insufficient historical data'\n        }\n\n    try:\n        # Fit Prophet model on pre-treatment data\n        model = Prophet(\n            daily_seasonality=False,\n            weekly_seasonality=True,\n            yearly_seasonality=True,\n            changepoint_prior_scale=0.05,\n            seasonality_prior_scale=10.0,\n            interval_width=0.95\n        )\n\n        model.fit(train_data)\n\n        # Make forecast for the entire period\n        future = model.make_future_dataframe(periods=31)  # Extend into March\n        forecast = model.predict(future)\n\n        # Get actual values for treatment period\n        treatment_data = df_prophet[\n            (df_prophet['ds'] >= treatment_start) &\n            (df_prophet['ds'] <= treatment_end)\n        ].copy()\n\n        # Get forecasted values for treatment period\n        forecast_treatment = forecast[\n            (forecast['ds'] >= treatment_start) &\n            (forecast['ds'] <= treatment_end)\n        ][['ds', 'yhat', 'yhat_lower', 'yhat_upper']].copy()\n\n        # Merge actual and forecast\n        merged = treatment_data.merge(forecast_treatment, on='ds', how='left')\n\n        if len(merged) == 0:\n            return {\n                'category': category_name,\n                'anomaly_index': 0,\n                'baseline_avg': 0,\n                'treatment_avg': 0,\n                'forecast_avg': 0,\n                'error': 'No data in treatment period'\n            }\n\n        # Calculate metrics\n        actual_avg = merged['y'].mean()\n        forecast_avg = merged['yhat'].mean()\n        baseline_avg = train_data['y'].mean()\n\n        # Calculate anomaly index\n        # Deviation from forecast, normalized by forecast std\n        deviation = actual_avg - forecast_avg\n        forecast_std = train_data['y'].std()\n\n        if forecast_std > 0:\n            # Standardized deviation\n            z_score = deviation / forecast_std\n            # Map to -100 to 100 scale using tanh for bounded range\n            anomaly_index = np.tanh(z_score / 2) * 100\n        else:\n            anomaly_index = 0\n\n        return {\n            'category': category_name,\n            'anomaly_index': anomaly_index,\n            'baseline_avg': baseline_avg,\n            'treatment_avg': actual_avg,\n            'forecast_avg': forecast_avg,\n            'deviation': deviation,\n            'z_score': z_score if forecast_std > 0 else 0\n        }\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\n            'category': category_name,\n            'anomaly_index': 0,\n            'baseline_avg': 0,\n            'treatment_avg': 0,\n            'forecast_avg': 0,\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"TIME SERIES ANOMALY DETECTION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Prepare data for Prophet (needs 'ds' and 'y' columns)\n    df_prophet = category_data[['ds', 'y']].copy()\n\n    # Filter to data BEFORE treatment period for training\n    train_data = df_prophet[df_prophet['ds'] < treatment_start].copy()\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n        actual_avg = merged['y'].mean()\n        forecast_avg = merged['yhat'].mean()\n        baseline_avg = train_data['y'].mean()\n\n        # Calculate anomaly index\n        # Deviation from forecast, normalized by forecast std\n        deviation = actual_avg - forecast_avg\n        forecast_std = train_data['y'].std()\n\n        if forecast_std > 0:\n            # Standardized deviation\n            z_score = deviation / forecast_std\n            # Map to -100 to 100 scale using tanh for bounded range\n            anomaly_index = np.tanh(z_score / 2) * 100\n        else:\n            anomaly_index = 0\n\n        return {\n            'category': category_name,\n            'anomaly_index': anomaly_index,\n            'baseline_avg': baseline_avg,\n            'treatment_avg': actual_avg,\n            'forecast_avg': forecast_avg,\n            'deviation': deviation,\n            'z_score': z_score if forecast_std > 0 else 0\n        }\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\n            'category': category_name,\n            'anomaly_index': 0,\n            'baseline_avg': 0,\n            'treatment_avg': 0,\n            'forecast_avg': 0,\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"TIME SERIES ANOMALY DETECTION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # analyze_category(category, intensive_df, extensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols,\n                     anomaly_df, purchase_df, baseline_start, baseline_end,\n                     treatment_start, treatment_end, category_type):\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze a single category using DiD for both intensive and extensive margins\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Get anomaly index\n    anomaly_index = anomaly_df[anomaly_df['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].values[0]\n\n    # Calculate baseline and treatment metrics\n    category_purchases = purchase_df[\n        (purchase_df['Category'] == category) &\n        (\n            ((purchase_df['Order Date'] >= baseline_start) & (purchase_df['Order Date'] <= baseline_end)) |\n            ((purchase_df['Order Date'] >= treatment_start) & (purchase_df['Order Date'] <= treatment_end))\n        )\n    ].copy()\n\n    baseline_purchases = category_purchases[\n        (category_purchases['Order Date'] >= baseline_start) &\n        (category_purchases['Order Date'] <= baseline_end)\n    ]\n    treatment_purchases = category_purchases[\n        (category_purchases['Order Date'] >= treatment_start) &\n        (category_purchases['Order Date'] <= treatment_end)\n    ]\n\n    baseline_avg_spend = baseline_purchases['Total_Spend'].mean() if len(baseline_purchases) > 0 else 0\n    treatment_avg_spend = treatment_purchases['Total_Spend'].mean() if len(treatment_purchases) > 0 else 0\n    n_purchasers_baseline = baseline_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n    n_purchasers_treatment = treatment_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n\n    n_at_risk = survey_features['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n    baseline_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_baseline / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n    treatment_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_treatment / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n\n    print(f\"  Anomaly Index: {anomaly_index:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Baseline avg spend: ${baseline_avg_spend:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Treatment avg spend: ${treatment_avg_spend:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Baseline purchasers: {n_purchasers_baseline}\")\n    print(f\"  Treatment purchasers: {n_purchasers_treatment}\")\n\n    # INTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    print(f\"\\n  Analyzing intensive margin (spending among purchasers)...\")\n    intensive_results = analyze_intensive_margin(\n        category, intensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type\n    )\n\n    # EXTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    print(f\"  Analyzing extensive margin (purchase propensity)...\")\n \"anomaly_index\": round(anomaly_index, 2),\n        \"baseline_avg_spend\": round(baseline_avg_spend, 2),\n        \"treatment_avg_spend\": round(treatment_avg_spend, 2),\n        \"n_purchasers_baseline\": int(n_purchasers_baseline),\n        \"n_purchasers_treatment\": int(n_purchasers_treatment),\n        \"baseline_purchase_rate\": round(baseline_purchase_rate, 4),\n extensive_results\n    }\n\n\ndef analyze_intensive_margin(category, intensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type):\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze intensive margin using univariate DiD for each feature\n    Returns top 3 drivers sorted by contribution strength\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to this category\n    category_data = intensive_df[intensive_df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(category_data) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Merge with survey features\n    merged = category_data.merge(survey_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n    # Run univariate DiD for each feature\n    results = []\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        try:\n            # Create treatment indicator (1 for treatment, 0 for baseline)\n            merged['treatment'] = (merged['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n            # Get feature values (should be binary 0/1)\n            if feature not in merged.columns:\n                continue\n\n            feature_values = merged[feature].fillna(0)\n\n            # Simple univariate DiD estimation\n            # Group by treatment and feature\n            groups = merged.groupby(['treatment', feature])['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n            if len(groups) < 4:  # Need all 4 combinations\n                continue\n\n            # DiD estimate\n            try:\n                treat_1 = groups.loc[(1, 1.0)] if (1, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                treat_0 = groups.loc[(1, 0.0)] if (1, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_1 = groups.loc[(0, 1.0)] if (0, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_0 = groups.loc[(0, 0.0)] if (0, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n\n                did_estimate = (treat_1 - treat_0) - (base_1 - base_0)\n\n                # Simple t-test approximation for p-value\n                n_obs = len(merged)\n                std_err = merged['Total_Spend'].std() / np.sqrt(n_obs) if n_obs > 0 else 1\n                t_stat = did_estimate / std_err if std_err > 0 else 0\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - 0.95) if abs(t_stat) > 1.96 else 0.5  # Approximate\n\n                results.append({\n                    'feature': feature,\n                    'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                    'p_value': p_value\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    # Sort by absolute DiD estimate (contribution strength)\n    # For surge categories: want largest positive contributions\n    # For slump categories: want largest negative contributions\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:  # slump\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    # Take top 3\n    top_3 = results_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Format results\n    formatted = []\n    for r in top_3:\n        formatted.append({\n            'feature': r['feature'],\n            'did_estimate': round(r['did_estimate'], 2),\n            'p_value': round(r['p_value'], 3),\n            'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n        })\n\n    return formatted\n\n\ndef analyze_extensive_margin(category, extensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type):\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze extensive margin using multivariate heterogeneous DiD\n    Returns top 3 drivers sorted by contribution strength\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to this category\n    category_data = extensive_df[extensive_df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(category_data) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Merge with survey features\n    merged = category_data.merge(survey_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n    # Run univariate DiD for each feature\n    results = []\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        try:\n            # Create treatment indicator\n            merged['treatment'] = (merged['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n            if feature not in merged.columns:\n                continue\n\n            feature_values = merged[feature].fillna(0)\n\n            # Simple univariate DiD estimation for binary outcome\n            groups = merged.groupby(['treatment', feature])['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n            if len(groups) < 4:\n                continue\n\n            try:\n                treat_1 = groups.loc[(1, 1.0)] if (1, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                treat_0 = groups.loc[(1, 0.0)] if (1, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_1 = groups.loc[(0, 1.0)] if (0, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_0 = groups.loc[(0, 0.0)] if (0, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n\n                did_estimate = (treat_1 - treat_0) - (base_1 - base_0)\n\n                # Simple approximation for p-value\n                n_obs = len(merged)\n                std_err = merged['Has_Purchase'].std() / np.sqrt(n_obs) if n_obs > 0 else 1\n                t_stat = did_estimate / std_err if std_err > 0 else 0\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - 0.95) if abs(t_stat) > 1.96 else 0.5\n\n                results.append({\n                    'feature': feature,\n                    'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                    'p_value': p_value\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    # Sort by contribution strength\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:  # slump\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    # Take top 3\n    top_3 = results_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Format results\n    formatted = []\n    for r in top_3:\n        formatted.append({\n            'feature': r['feature'],\n            'did_estimate': round(r['did_estimate'], 4),\n            'p_value': round(r['p_value'], 3),\n            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n        })\n\n    return formatted\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    results = (category_purchases['Order Date'] <= treatment_end)\n    ]\n\n    baseline_avg_spend = baseline_purchases['Total_Spend'].mean() if len(baseline_purchases) > 0 else 0\n    treatment_avg_spend = ${baseline_avg_spend:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Treatment avg spend: ${treatment_avg_spend:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Baseline purchasers: {n_purchasers_baseline}\")\n    print(f\"  Treatment purchasers: for each feature\n    results = []\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        try:\n            # Create treatment indicator (1 for treatment, 0 for baseline)\n            merged['treatment'] = (merged['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n            # Get feature values (should be binary 0/1)\n            if feature not in merged.columns:\n                continue\n\n            feature_values = merged[feature].fillna(0)\n\n            # Simple univariate DiD estimation\n            # Group by treatment and feature\n            groups = merged.groupby(['treatment', feature])['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n            if len(groups) < 4:  # Need all 4 combinations\n                continue\n\n            # DiD estimate\n            try:\n                treat_1 = groups.loc[(1, 1.0)] if (1, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                treat_0 = groups.loc[(1, 0.0)] if (1, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_1 = groups.loc[(0, 1.0)] if (0, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_0 = groups.loc[(0, 0.0)] if (0, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n\n                did_estimate = (treat_1 - treat_0) - (base_1 - base_0)\n\n                # Simple t-test approximation for p-value\n                n_obs = len(merged)\n                std_err = merged['Total_Spend'].std() / np.sqrt(n_obs) if n_obs > 0 else 1\n                t_stat = did_estimate / std_err if std_err > 0 else 0\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - 0.95) if abs(t_stat) > 1.96 else 0.5  # Approximate\n\n                results.append({\n                    'feature': feature,\n                    'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                    'p_value': p_value\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    # Sort by absolute DiD estimate (contribution strength)\n    # For surge categories: want largest positive contributions\n    # For slump categories: want largest negative contributions\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:  # slump\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    # Take top 3\n    top_3 = results_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Format results\n    formatted = []\n    for r in top_3:\n {n_purchasers_treatment}\")\n\n    # INTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    print(f\"\\n  Analyzing intensive margin (spending among purchasers)...\")\n    intensive_results = analyze_intensive_margin(\n        category, intensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type\n    )\n\n    # EXTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    print(f\"  Analyzing extensive margin (purchase propensity)...\")\n    extensive_results = analyze_extensive_margin(\n        category, extensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type\n    )\n\n    return {\n        \"category\": category,\n        \"anomaly_index\": round(anomaly_index, 2),\n        \"baseline_avg_spend\": round(baseline_avg_spend, 2),\n        \"treatment_avg_spend\": round(treatment_avg_spend, 2),\n        \"n_purchasers_baseline\": int(n_purchasers_baseline),\n        \"n_purchasers_treatment\": int(n_purchasers_treatment),\n        \"baseline_purchase_rate\": round(baseline_purchase_rate, 4),\n        \"treatment_purchase_rate\": round(treatment_purchase_rate, 4),\n        \"n_at_risk\": int(n_at_risk),\n        \"intensive_margin\": intensive_results,\n        \"extensive_margin\": extensive_results\n    }\n\n\ndef analyze_intensive_margin(category, intensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type):\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze intensive margin using univariate DiD for each feature\n    Returns top 3 drivers sorted by contribution strength\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to this category\n    category_data = intensive_df[intensive_df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(category_data) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Merge with survey features\n    merged = category_data.merge(survey_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n    # Run univariate DiD for each feature\n    results = []\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        try:\n            # Create treatment indicator (1 for treatment, 0 for baseline)\n            merged['treatment'] = (merged['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n            # Get feature values (should be binary 0/1)\n            if feature not in merged.columns:\n                continue\n\n            feature_values = merged[feature].fillna(0)\n\n            # Simple univariate DiD estimation\n            # Group by treatment and feature\n            groups = merged.groupby(['treatment', feature])['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n            if len(groups) < 4:  # Need all 4 combinations\n                continue\n\n            # DiD estimate\n            try:\n                treat_1 = groups.loc[(1, 1.0)] if (1, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                treat_0 = groups.loc[(1, 0.0)] if (1, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_1 = groups.loc[(0, 1.0)] if (0, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_0 = groups.loc[(0, 0.0)] if (0, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n\n                did_estimate = (treat_1 - treat_0) - (base_1 - base_0)\n\n                # Simple t-test approximation for p-value\n                n_obs = len(merged)\n                std_err = merged['Total_Spend'].std() / np.sqrt(n_obs) if n_obs > 0 else 1\n                t_stat = did_estimate / std_err if std_err > 0 else 0\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - 0.95) if abs(t_stat) > 1.96 else 0.5  # Approximate\n\n                results.append({\n                    'feature': feature,\n                    'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                    'p_value': p_value\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    # Sort by absolute DiD estimate (contribution strength)\n    # For surge categories: want largest positive contributions\n    # For slump categories: want largest negative contributions\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:  # slump\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    # Take top 3\n    top_3 = results_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Format results\n    formatted = []\n    for r in top_3:\n        formatted.append({\n            'feature': r['feature'],\n            'did_estimate': round(r['did_estimate'], 2),\n            'p_value': round(r['p_value'], 3),\n            'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n        })\n\n    return formatted\n\n\ndef analyze_extensive_margin(category, extensive_df, survey_features, feature_cols, category_type):\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze extensive margin using multivariate heterogeneous DiD\n    Returns top 3 drivers sorted by contribution strength\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to this category\n    category_data = extensive_df[extensive_df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(category_data) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Merge with survey features\n    merged = category_data.merge(survey_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n estimation for binary outcome\n            groups = merged.groupby(['treatment', feature])['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n            if len(groups) < 4:\n                continue\n\n            try:\n                treat_1 = groups.loc[(1, 1.0)] if (1, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                treat_0 = groups.loc[(1, 0.0)] if (1, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_1 = groups.loc[(0, 1.0)] if (0, 1.0) in groups.index else 0\n                base_0 = groups.loc[(0, 0.0)] if (0, 0.0) in groups.index else 0\n\n                did_estimate = (treat_1 - treat_0) - (base_1 - base_0)\n\n                # Simple approximation for p-value\n                n_obs = len(merged)\n                std_err = merged['Has_Purchase'].std() / np.sqrt(n_obs) if n_obs > 0 else 1\n                t_stat = did_estimate / std_err if std_err > 0 else 0\n                p_value = 2 * (1 - 0.95) if abs(t_stat) > 1.96 else 0.5\n\n                results.append({\n                    'feature': feature,\n                    'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                    'p_value': p_value\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        except Exception as Filter to category\n    cat_data = margin_df[margin_df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Merge with features\n    merged = cat_data.merge(survey_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n    # Create treatment indicator\n    merged['treat'] = (merged['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n    # Analyze each feature\n    results = []\n\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        if feature not in merged.columns:\n            continue\n\n        # Get feature values (binary)\n        merged[feature] = merged[feature].fillna(0).astype(float)\n\n        # Skip if no variation\n        if merged[feature].std() == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate group means\n        try:\n            # Group by treatment and feature\n            group_means = merged.groupby(['treat', feature])[outcome_col].agg(['mean', 'count'])\n\n            # Need all 4 groups\n            if len(group_means) < 4:\n                continue\n\n            # Extract means (with defaults)\n            treat_1_mean = group_means.loc[(1, 1.0), 'mean'] if (1, 1.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            treat_0_mean = group_means.loc[(1, 0.0), 'mean'] if (1, 0.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            base_1_mean = group_means.loc[(0, 1.0), 'mean'] if (0, 1.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            base_0_mean = group_means.loc[(0, 0.0), 'mean'] if (0, 0.0) in group_means.index else 0\n\n            # Extract counts\n            treat_1_n = group_means.loc[(1, 1.0), 'count'] if (1, 1.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            treat_0_n = group_means.loc[(1, 0.0), 'count'] if (1, 0.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            base_1_n = group_means.loc[(0, 1.0), 'count'] if (0, 1.0) in group_means.index else 0\n            base_0_n = group_means.loc[(0, 0.0), 'count'] if (0, 0.0) in group_means.index else 0\n\n            # Skip if any group is too small\n            if min(treat_1_n, treat_0_n, base_1_n, base_0_n) < 3:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate DiD estimate\n            did_estimate = (treat_1_mean - treat_0_mean) - (base_1_mean - base_0_mean)\n\n            # Calculate standard error (simplified)\n            pooled_std = merged.groupby(['treat', feature])[outcome_col].std().mean()\n            n_total = len(merged)\n            se = pooled_std / np.sqrt(n_total) if n_total > 0 else 1\n\n            # T-statistic and p-value\n            t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n            p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.t.cdf(abs(t_stat), df=n_total-4)) if n_total > 4 else 0.5\n\n            results.append({\n                'feature': feature,\n                'did_estimate': did_estimate,\n                'p_value': p_value,\n                't_stat': t_stat\n            })\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            continue\n\n    # Sort by contribution strength\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:\n        results_sorted = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    # Take top 3\n    top_3 = results_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Format\n    method = \"Univariate DiD\" if margin_type == 'intensive' else \"Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD\"\n    formatted = []\n    for r in top_3:\n        formatted.append({\n            'feature': r['feature'],\n            'did_estimate': round(r['did_estimate'], 2) if margin_type == 'intensive' else round(r['did_estimate'], 4),\n            'p_value': round(r['p_value'], 3),\n            'method': method\n        })\n\n    return formatted\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    results = run_did_analysis()\n    print(\"\\nAnalysis complete!\")\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":324,"cache_read_input_tokens":68873,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":324,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"29be4f7a-89f9-48ae-a013-68ce3a2abfd6","uuid":"e7c14ea1-723d-4cbc-aadf-7777f2eab689"}
